Extract PO number (line after \"PO Number:\")\n            po_match = re.search(r'PO Number:\\s*([A-Z0-9\\-]+)', text)\n            if po_match:\n                invoice['po_number'] = po_match.group(1).strip()\n\n            invoices.append(invoice)\n\n    return invoices\n\ndef fuzzy_match_vendor(invoice_vendor: str, vendors_df: pd.DataFrame, threshold: int = 85) -> Optional[pd.Series]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Find matching vendor using fuzzy string matching.\n    Returns vendor record if match found, None otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not invoice_vendor:\n        return None\n\n    best_match = None\n    best_score = 0\n\n    for _, vendor in vendors_df.iterrows():\n        vendor_name = str(vendor['Name'])\n        score = fuzz.ratio(invoice_vendor.lower(), vendor_name.lower())\n\n        if score > best_score:\n            best_score = score\n            best_match = vendor\n\n    # Return match if score exceeds threshold\n    if best_score >= threshold:\n        return best_match\n    return None\n\ndef check_invoice_fraud(invoice: Dict, vendors_df: pd.DataFrame, pos_df: pd.DataFrame) -> Optional[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Check if invoice is fraudulent based on criteria.\n    Returns fraud record if fraudulent, None otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    vendor_name = invoice['vendor_name']\n    invoice_amount = invoice['invoice_amount']\n    iban = invoice['iban']\n    po_number = invoice['po_number']\n\n    # Criteria 1: Unknown Vendor\n    matched_vendor = fuzzy_match_vendor(vendor_name, vendors_df)\n    if not matched_vendor:\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'Unknown Vendor'\n        }\n\n    # Criteria 2: IBAN Mismatch\n    if iban != matched_vendor['IBAN']:\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'IBAN Mismatch'\n        }\n\n    # Criteria 3: Invalid PO (only if PO number is present)\n    if po_number:\n        po_record = pos_df[pos_df['PO Number'] == po_number]\n        if po_record.empty:\n            return {\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                'iban': iban,\n                'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'Invalid PO'\n            }\n\n        po_record = po_record.iloc[0]\n\n        # Criteria 4: Amount Mismatch (difference exceeds 0.01)\n        amount_diff = abs(invoice_amount - po_record['Amount'])\n        if amount_diff > 0.01:\n            return {\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                'iban': iban,\n                'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'Amount Mismatch'\n            }\n\n        # Criteria 5: Vendor Mismatch\n        if matched_vendor['Vendor ID'] != po_record['Vendor ID']:\n            return {\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                'iban': iban,\n                'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'Vendor Mismatch'\n            }\n\n    # No fraud detected\n    return None\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading vendor data...\")\n    vendors_df = pd.read_excel('/root/vendors.xlsx')\n\n    print(\"Loading purchase orders...\")\n    pos_df = pd.read_csv('/root/purchase_orders.csv')\n\n    print(\"Extracting invoice data from PDF...\")\n    invoices = extract_invoice_data('/root/invoices.pdf')\n    print(f\"Found {len(invoices)} invoices\")\n\n    print(\"Analyzing invoices for fraud...\")\n    fraudulent_invoices = []\n\n    for invoice in invoices:\n        fraud_record = check_invoice_fraud(invoice, vendors_df, pos_df)\n        if fraud_record:\n            fraudulent_invoices.append(fraud_record)\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(fraudulent_invoices)} fraudulent invoices\")\n\n    # Save to JSON\n    output_path = '/root/fraud_report.json'\n    with open(output_path, 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(fraudulent_invoices, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Fraud report saved to {output_path}\")\n\n    # invoices.append(invoice)\n\n    return invoices\n\ndef fuzzy_match_vendor(invoice_vendor: str, vendors_df: pd.DataFrame, threshold: int = 85) -> Optional[pd.Series]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Find matching vendor using fuzzy string matching.\n    Returns vendor record if match found, None otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not invoice_vendor:\n        return None\n\n    best_match = None\n    best_score = 0\n\n    for _, vendor in vendors_df.iterrows():\n        vendor_name = str(vendor['Name'])\n        score = fuzz.ratio(invoice_vendor.lower(), vendor_name.lower())\n\n        if score > best_score:\n            best_score = score\n            best_match = vendor\n\n    # Return match if score exceeds threshold\n    if best_score >= threshold:\n        return best_match\n    return None\n\ndef check_invoice_fraud(invoice: Dict, vendors_df: pd.DataFrame, pos_df: pd.DataFrame) -> Optional[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Check if invoice is fraudulent based on criteria.\n    Returns fraud record if            'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'Invalid PO'\n            }\n\n        po_record = po_record.iloc[0]\n\n        # Criteria 4: Amount Mismatch (difference exceeds 0.01)\n        amount_diff = abs(invoice_amount - po_record['Amount'])\n        if amount_diff > 0.01:\n            return {\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                'iban': iban,\n                'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'Vendor Mismatch'\n            }\n\n    # No fraud detected\n    return None\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading vendor Extract vendor name (line after \"Vendor:\")\n            vendor_match = re.search(r'Vendor:\\s*(.+?)(?:\\n|$)', text)\n            if vendor_match:\n                invoice['vendor_name'] = vendor_match.group(1).strip()\n\n            # Extract amount (line after \"Amount:\")\n            amount_match = re.search(r'Amount:\\s*\\$?\\s*([\\d,]+\\.?\\d*)', text)\n            if amount_match:\n                amount_str = amount_match.group(1).replace(',', '')\n                invoice['invoice_amount'] = float(amount_str)\n\n            # Extract IBAN (line after \"IBAN:\")\n            iban_match = re.search(r'IBAN:\\s*([A-Z0-9]+)', text)\n            if iban_match:\n                invoice['iban'] = iban_match.group(1).strip()\n\n            # Extract PO number (line after \"PO Number:\")\n            po_match = re.search(r'PO Number:\\s*([A-Z0-9\\-]+)', text)\n            if po_match:\n                invoice['po_number'] = po_match.group(1).strip()\n\n            invoices.append(invoice)\n\n    return invoices\n\ndef fuzzy_match_vendor(invoice_vendor: str, vendors_df: pd.DataFrame, threshold: int = 85) -> Optional[pd.Series]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Find matching vendor using fuzzy string matching.\n    Returns vendor record if match found, None otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not invoice_vendor:\n        return None\n\n    best_match = None\n    best_score = 0\n\n    for _, vendor in vendors_df.iterrows():\n        vendor_name = str(vendor['Name'])\n        score = fuzz.ratio(invoice_vendor.lower(), vendor_name.lower())\n\n        if score > best_score:\n            best_score = score\n            best_match = vendor\n\n    # Criteria 1: Unknown Vendor\n    matched_vendor = fuzzy_match_vendor(vendor_name, vendors_df)\n    if not matched_vendor:\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'Unknown Vendor'\n        }\n\n    # Criteria 2: IBAN Mismatch\n    if iban != matched_vendor['IBAN']:\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'IBAN Mismatch'\n        }\n\n    # Criteria 3: Invalid PO (only if PO number is present)\n    if po_number:\n        po_record = pos_df[pos_df['PO Number'] == po_number]\n        if po_record.empty:\n            return {\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                'iban': iban,\n                'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'Invalid PO'\n            }\n\n        po_record = po_record.iloc[0]\n\n        # Criteria 4: Amount Mismatch (difference exceeds 0.01)\n        amount_diff = abs(invoice_amount - po_record['Amount'])\n        if amount_diff > 0.01:\n            return {\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                'iban': iban,\n                'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'Amount Mismatch'\n            }\n\n        # Criteria 5: Vendor Mismatch\n        if matched_vendor['Vendor ID'] != po_record['Vendor ID']:\n            return {\n                'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n                'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n                'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n                'iban': iban,\n                'po_number': po_number,\n                'reason': 'Vendor Mismatch'\n            }\n\n    # No fraud detected\n    return None\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading vendor data...\")\n    vendors_df = pd.read_excel('/root/vendors.xlsx')\n\n    print(\"Loading purchase orders...\")\n    pos_df = pd.read_csv('/root/purchase_orders.csv')\n\n    print(\"Extracting invoice data from PDF...\")\n    invoices = extract_invoice_data('/root/invoices.pdf')\n    print(f\"Found {len(invoices)} invoices\")\n\n    print(\"Analyzing invoices for fraud...\")\n    fraudulent_invoices = []\n\n    for invoice in invoices:\n        fraud_record = check_invoice_fraud(invoice, vendors_df, pos_df)\n        if fraud_record:\n            fraudulent_invoices.append(fraud_record)\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(fraudulent_invoices)} fraudulent invoices\")\n\n    # Save to JSON\n    output_path = '/root/fraud_report.json'\n    with open(output_path, 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(fraudulent_invoices, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Fraud report saved to {output_path}\")\n\n    # Extract invoice details using regex patterns\n            invoice = {\n                'invoice_page_number': page_num + 1,  # 1-based indexing\n                'vendor_name': None,\n                'invoice_amount': None,\n                'iban': None,\n                'po_number': None\n            }\n\n            # Extract vendor name (line after \"From:\")\n            vendor_match = re.search(r'From:\\s*(.+?)(?:\\n|$)', text)\n            if vendor_match:\n                invoice['vendor_name'] = vendor_match.group(1).strip()\n\n            # Extract IBAN (line after \"Payment IBAN:\")\n            iban_match = re.search(r'Payment IBAN:\\s*([A-Z0-9_]+)', text)\n            if iban_match:\n                invoice['iban'] = iban_match.group(1).strip()\n\n            #
